# Organisation & Company - endpoint reference

> Company registration/profile, legal entities, branches, departments, designations, grades, shifts, holiday & work calendars, policy assignments, dashboard.

Generated by [`tools/build_api_docs.py`](../tools/build_api_docs.py) from the running FastAPI app (routes, dependencies, Pydantic schemas) - not hand-written. Examples are **schema-derived and illustrative; they were not executed against a server** unless a section says otherwise. Common conventions (envelope, pagination, headers, errors) are in [API_OVERVIEW.md](../API_OVERVIEW.md) and [ERROR_HANDLING.md](../ERROR_HANDLING.md); the models linked below are in [models/organisation.md](../models/organisation.md).

## Domain error codes raised by this module (47)

Codes below are defined in this module's exception classes. Which endpoint raises which code is not statically mapped by this document (it depends on service logic); treat the list as the module's vocabulary and branch on `error.code`. Full catalogue: [ERROR_CODES.md](../ERROR_CODES.md).

| Status | Code | Class | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 404 | `SHIFT_VERSION_NOT_FOUND_FOR_DATE` | `ShiftVersionNotFoundForDateError` | `GET /shifts/{id}/versions/resolve?as_of=` found no PUBLISHED version covering that date. |
| 409 | `BRANCH_HAS_ACTIVE_DEPARTMENTS` | `BranchDeactivationBlockedError` | FR-ORG-014 — never hard-delete; deactivation is blocked while active Department rows still reference this branch. (The Employee half of FR-ORG-014 is out of scope: Employee does not exist yet.) |
| 409 | `CALENDAR_EXCEPTION_DATE_CONFLICT` | `CalendarExceptionDateConflictError` | `calendar_exception` carries a partial unique index on `(work_calendar_id, exception_date)` for active rows (migration `20260818120000_work_calendar`) — at most one active exception per date per calendar. Correcting one is deactivate-then-i |
| 409 | `COMPANY_LIMIT_REACHED` | `CompanyLimitReachedError` | Initial release allows a single company per tenant (any number of branches). |
| 409 | `DEPARTMENT_CYCLE_DETECTED` | `DepartmentCycleDetectedError` | FR-ORG-005 — the department hierarchy must never contain a cycle. Raised when the candidate `parent_id` is the department itself or one of its own descendants. |
| 409 | `DEPARTMENT_MAX_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` | `DepartmentMaxDepthExceededError` | FR-ORG-005 — the department hierarchy is capped at 5 levels. |
| 409 | `GRADE_CODE_ALREADY_EXISTS` | `GradeCodeAlreadyExistsError` | The `grade` table already carries a `UNIQUE (company_id, code)` constraint (predates this slice, scripts/sql/001_init_schema.sql) — this converts the resulting `IntegrityError` into the documented error envelope rather than a raw 500, mirro |

| 409 | `HOLIDAY_DATE_ALREADY_EXISTS` | `HolidayDateAlreadyExistsError` | `holiday` carries a partial unique index on `(calendar_id, date)` for active rows (migration `20260818100000_holiday_expand`) — at most one active holiday per date per calendar. |
| 409 | `LEGAL_ENTITY_HAS_ACTIVE_BRANCHES` | `LegalEntityDeactivationBlockedError` | FR-ORG-014 — never hard-delete; deactivation is blocked while active Branch rows still reference this legal entity. |

| 409 | `SHIFT_VERSION_NOT_DRAFT` | `ShiftVersionNotDraftError` | PATCH/DELETE/publish against a `ShiftVersion` that is no longer `DRAFT` — published versions are immutable except for `effective_to`, which only the publish flow itself sets. |
| 409 | `SHIFT_VERSION_OVERLAP` | `ShiftVersionOverlapError` | `shift_version_no_overlap` (GiST exclusion) is the race-condition backstop — converts the resulting `IntegrityError` into the documented envelope, mirroring `PolicyAssignmentOverlapError`. |

<a id="get-v1-branches"></a>

**List Branches**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `GET /v1/branches` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |

| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Admin preview of the WD-09 precedence walk for one employee+date (architecture §10).

deferred to W4, since no `ATTENDANCE.*` permission codes exist yet (WD-19).

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Public, unauthenticated — read-only. Lets the sign-up page (and the company-profile edit
screen) narrow their timezone `<select>` to the server's actual allow-list instead of a
second, hand-maintained TypeScript copy (execution gate §6.3). No rate limit beyond the
default: this returns no tenant data and cannot be used to enumerate or affect anything.

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X GET "$BASE_URL/v1/companies/registration-config"
```

## GET /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/resolve
<a id="get-v1-shifts-shift-id-versions-resolve"></a>

**Resolve Shift Version**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `GET /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/resolve` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ORG.SHIFT.MANAGE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/policy_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py:749`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py#L749) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Registered ahead of `/versions/{version_id}` — a literal path segment must be matched
before the parameterized route, or `as_of` would be parsed as a `version_id` UUID and
404/422 instead of reaching this handler.

| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

## DELETE /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/{version_id}
<a id="delete-v1-shifts-shift-id-versions-version-id"></a>

**Discard Shift Version**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `DELETE /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/{version_id}` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ORG.SHIFT.MANAGE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/policy_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py:802`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py#L802) |
| Automated tests | path-only: `test_attendance_day.py`, `test_break_cross_midnight.py`, `test_regularization.py` (+4) |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Discards a DRAFT only (409 `SHIFT_VERSION_NOT_DRAFT` once published) — soft-deletes via
`deleted_at`, never a hard delete; returns the discarded resource rather than 204, matching
this API's envelope convention (no bare-204 response exists elsewhere in this module).

| `shift_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `version_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `If-Match` | header | yes | string | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

```bash
  -H "If-Match: <resource version>"
```

## POST /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/{version_id}/publish
<a id="post-v1-shifts-shift-id-versions-version-id-publish"></a>

**Publish Shift Version**

### Endpoint overview

| | |
|---|---|
| Method / path | `POST /v1/shifts/{shift_id}/versions/{version_id}/publish` |
| Authentication | Tenant Bearer |
| Required permission(s) | `ORG.SHIFT.MANAGE` |
| Rate limit | none declared on this route (a global middleware only covers the onboarding upload path) |
| Idempotency-Key | not used |
| Success status | 200 |
| Mobile suitability | **C** - Admin / back-office configuration (permission-gated) |
| Referenced by Flutter app | `features/attendance/data/policy_repository.dart` |
| Source | [`apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py:827`](../../../apps/api/src/hrms/modules/organisation/router.py#L827) |
| Automated tests | direct: `test_shift_version_pagination.py` |

**Purpose / behaviour (from source docstring):**

Two concurrency tokens: `If-Match` is the draft's own (`expected_version`, this
endpoint's normal optimistic-concurrency check); `body.expected_previous_version` is the
currently open PUBLISHED version's token, required only when one exists — mirrors
`create_policy_assignment`'s insert-new/close-previous flow exactly, just with two rows
being mutated instead of one. `ShiftVersionOverlapError` (409) is the GiST-exclusion
race-condition backstop if another request published a conflicting date range in the
interim.

### Request specification - parameters

| Name | In | Required | Type | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `shift_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `version_id` | path | yes | string(uuid) | - | - |
| `If-Match` | header | yes | string | - | - |
| `X-Company-Id` | header | no | string | - | - |

### Request body (`application/json`, required)

Schema: [ShiftVersionPublishRequest](../models/organisation.md#shiftversionpublishrequest)

| Field | Required | Type | Nullable | Constraints / default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `publish_reason` | no | string | yes | - | - |
| `expected_previous_version` | no | integer | yes | - | - |

### Request example (schema-derived, not executed)

```bash
curl -X POST "$BASE_URL/v1/shifts/<shift_id>/versions/<version_id>/publish" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "If-Match: <resource version>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"publish_reason":"Example publish reason","expected_previous_version":1}'
```
